# the goods whose total changed: their building types in each state (one building per type and state)
spec_bp_direct = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { local_var:spec_bpt_small_arms = 1 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			b:building_arms_industry ?= {
				if = {
					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_small_arms = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_small_arms } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_small_arms }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_small_arms } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_small_arms }
					if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_small_arms > 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_up_small_arms multiplier = local_var:spec_bpv_small_arms } }
					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_small_arms < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_small_arms multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_small_arms multiply = -1 } } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { local_var:spec_bpt_fine_art = 1 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			b:building_art_academy ?= {
				if = {
					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_fine_art = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_fine_art } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_fine_art }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_fine_art } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_fine_art }
					if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_fine_art > 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_up_fine_art multiplier = local_var:spec_bpv_fine_art } }
					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_fine_art < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_fine_art multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_fine_art multiply = -1 } } }

					if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_automobiles > 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_up_automobiles multiplier = local_var:spec_bpv_automobiles } }
					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_automobiles < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_automobiles multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_automobiles multiply = -1 } } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_aeroplanes = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_aeroplanes } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_aeroplanes }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_aeroplanes } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_aeroplanes }

					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_dye = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_dye } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_dye }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_dye } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_dye }
					if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_dye > 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_up_dye multiplier = local_var:spec_bpv_dye } }
					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_dye < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_dye multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_dye multiply = -1 } } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_silk = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_silk } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_silk }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_silk } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_silk }
					if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_silk > 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_up_silk multiplier = local_var:spec_bpv_silk } }
					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_silk < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_silk multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_silk multiply = -1 } } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { local_var:spec_bpt_tea = 1 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			b:building_tea_plantation ?= {
				if = {
					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_tea = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_tea } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_tea }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_tea } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_tea }

					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_tea < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_tea multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_tea multiply = -1 } } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { local_var:spec_bpt_clothes = 1 local_var:spec_bpt_luxury_clothes = 1 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			b:building_textile_mill ?= {
				if = {

					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_clothes < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_clothes multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_clothes multiply = -1 } } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { local_var:spec_bpt_luxury_clothes = 1 }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_up_luxury_clothes } remove_modifier = spec_bp_up_luxury_clothes }
					if = { limit = { has_modifier = spec_bp_down_luxury_clothes } remove_modifier = spec_bp_down_luxury_clothes }
					if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_luxury_clothes > 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_up_luxury_clothes multiplier = local_var:spec_bpv_luxury_clothes } }
					else_if = { limit = { local_var:spec_bpv_luxury_clothes < 0 } add_modifier = { name = spec_bp_down_luxury_clothes multiplier = { value = local_var:spec_bpv_luxury_clothes multiply = -1 } } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { local_var:spec_bpt_tobacco = 1 } }
		every_scope_state = {
			b:building_tobacco_plantation ?= {
				if = {

	if = {
